# Beta 71 A6 integration

## Outcome and limits

Private branch: `astra/b71-a6-integrate`, based on C5 `a142739b`. Private Git directory: `.scratch/git-a6`. No push or emulator launch.

C5 metadata is applied and all four requested heads are merged in order. The combined colour/Arrowhead field writer is integrated. Registry: **176 total, 102 2K5, 73 APF**, plus one capability for the third game. The validation plan is **176 / 171 covered / 5 deferred / 129 distinct validators**.

**Production manifest BLOCKED:** `/media/noah/Storage` is mounted read-only in this session. The exact regeneration launcher failed at directory creation with `Errno 30` / “Read-only file system”. No disc copy was placed elsewhere. The runnable external command is `bash reports/b71_a6/manifest_regen.sh`. The committed manifest is a clearly labelled bounded XBE projection, **not a production manifest or build receipt**.

**Disc PREPARED_NOT_RUN:** `reports/b71_a6/build_testdisc71.py` was prepared and statically checked, never imported or executed. Name: `NFL 2K5 MOD TEST 2026-09-15i (everything: scorebug v3 + colour + day tuning + modern Arrowhead + widescreen)`. Advanced (`softdrink_advanced`) with `scorebug=True`, `scorebug_runtime=True`, `modern_color=True`, `modern_arrowhead=True`, `widescreen=True`. It checks output access before pruning, retains every patch archive, removes oldest MOD TEST images until fewer than three remain, refuses existing named outputs, verifies every option, and exports a patch only after disc read-back. An actual build receipt and played result remain unavailable.

**PROVED:** exact registry preservation and wiring audit, frozen source fingerprints, the checks listed below, and 9 completed bounded combined-bundle proofs. **UNWITNESSED:** disc construction, boot, GPU filtering, real-match scorebug transitions, final stadium/colour appearance, APF runtime behavior, and platform-specific installation outside the executed local closure. Native execution and decoded-byte proofs do not establish played behavior.

## Merge order and every conflict

1. Applied C5's four prose fields to `nfl2k5.presentation.modern_color_lighting` from `reports/b71_c5/registry-metadata.json`, before any merge. No new row.
2. S3 `464423f0`: root report/message conflicts take that job's handoff, archived before the next merge. The 2K5 changelog keeps all distinct C5/C4/v2/v3 bullets. S3 supplies the complete v3 wing/font/capsule implementation. Registry changes merge by ID without deleting rows.
3. Arrowhead `4819ab5b`: changelog keeps all bullets. `mod_build.py` keeps both colour recipe validation/replay and Arrowhead's option/preflight/build steps. `nfl2k5_build_settings.py` saves both settings. `build_panel_qt.py` keeps the colour controls and Arrowhead checkbox/plan value. Provider/runtime pin conflicts start from the integrated side, followed by module-closure reconciliation and repinning. The reviewed PNG catalogs merge all entries and their catalog SHA is recalculated in the release checker.
4. APF-4 `c6b0ce23`: root report/message/wiring conflicts take the job handoff and archive it before APF-5. Registry and APF runtime changes merge cleanly, retaining the situation-mask owner and evidence. An initial explicit staging attempt refused ignored research paths; a narrowly explicit force-add staged only the merged research paths, then the merge committed. An attempted APF-5 merge during that unfinished merge was refused and retried after APF-4 committed.
5. APF-5 `0edcb323`: root report/message/wiring archived. APF changelog and allowlist preserve both branches' distinct entries. Launcher conflicts retain both `situations` and `fourth_down` handlers and synchronize all four managed patch kinds. APF runtime uses 73 cards; all shared totals become 176. The phase1 conflict retains 102 2K5 capabilities and updates the shared total. The registry union is exact, not concatenated duplicate objects.

Each job's root report and wiring is retained as `ASTRA_B71_<JOB>_REPORT.md` / `WIRING_B71_<JOB>.md`, with reporter names anonymized. C5's base handoff is also retained. S3 and Arrowhead carry inherited T1 wiring; it is labelled historical, rather than reapplied as new work. APF-4 and APF-5 directly implemented their protected wiring.

**Arrowhead report discrepancy:** commit `4819ab5b` contains an inherited beta-70 A3 root report, not a dedicated Arrowhead report or disc placeholder. Its actual implementation report is `docs/modern_arrowhead/README.md`. The archived root copy has an explicit preparation-only disc status; the prepared builder can replace that line with a real receipt after an external successful build. No receipt was invented.

## Integration corrections

- Both colour and Arrowhead originally replaced the same compressed field scene; simply sequencing them would classify the second write as foreign. The integrated path paints the decoded Arrowhead textures first, then applies the selected colour recipe and compresses the shared field once inside its unchanged retail wrapper. Stadium art retains its separate applied pins.
- The combined writer preflights all nine variants before writing, requires a verified colour target and exact original retail source, and reparses both complete bundle and per-site hashes. It saves a shared receipt, supports same-recipe replay, and refuses changed bytes. It also explicitly opens the standalone Arrowhead destination writable.
- Each completed real-bundle proof checks that all decoded bytes outside the authored art retain C5's exact colour result, authored pixel indices survive, field wrapper bytes are retail, stadium art matches its applied pin, and every other bundle byte equals the colour result. The actual write/receipt/replay/tamper path is exercised through an in-memory archive using those freshly compiled bytes. No retail bundle is written to a report or fixture.
- Both APF profiles also pass a cross-owner audit with 48 situation policies: 4,094 situation-mask words and 37 fourth-down words have disjoint byte footprints, commute in either application order, and respect the full mask reservations. This is an authored-byte composition proof, not runtime behavior.
- Provider closure grows from 286 to 287 modules for the now-reachable Arrowhead writer; exact set/hash checks remain in place. Product catalog tuple `(102, 80, 8, 1, 0, 10, 3)` and its exact ID set retain the Arrowhead entry.
- Strict validation initially found 75 inherited evidence files missing despite the supplied hydration expectation. Exact independent copies were restored from their recorded read-only local sources and checked against the prior SHA-256 inventory. They are not bundled. No validator was weakened.

## Manifest receipt

- Last product code/data change: `data/nfl2k5_cave_reservations.json`, after repinning all merged writers. Subsequent report/receipt work does not change product sources.
- Kind: **bounded complete forward XBE projection**, inherited historical retail reservations plus freshly observed current owner bytes. `release_manifest=false`, `disc_built=false`, `production_regeneration_required=true`.
- SHA-256: `bbc7b1afd93bd4e0c7cabb02452d54e7ac05e6fe6e0d26d4ffdaf381a5368315`; bytes: 4884009; reservations: 13089; source seals: 337; observed writer steps: 138.
- Composed XBE SHA-256: `015649d4df9a2b97940d7af275f6bdc333a17a96839c89de22afcc916534b6f7`.
- Projection elapsed: 357.683 seconds, exit 0. Full production regeneration launcher: 0.005 seconds, exit 1. See `reports/b71_a6/manifest-receipt.json`, `manifest-projection.log`, `manifest-production.log`, and `storage-probe.log`.
- S3 owner revision 7 retains `CODE_SIZE=1408`, 128 writable data bytes and 16-byte alignments. A6 remeasured 1,386 used code bytes; S3 reports 413,568 appended resource bytes (0.394409 MiB). It adds 11,008 bytes over A5. The appended-volume measurement is inherited; this run rechecks its suites and owner composition. Arrowhead adds no XBE requests/caves.
- S3's native raster still differs from the photographed broadcast; integration does not claim pixel-exact acceptance or fix the later v3 visual residuals.

### Skips and preliminary failures

Skipped cases are not proof: final-test_apf_b661_ladder: 1, final-scorebug-layout: 6, final-test_nfl2k5_scorebug_fonts: 5, final-test_apf_b67_static_audit: 1, final-test_nfl2k5_scorebug_assets: 1, final-test_apf_b69_control_audit: 1, final-test_nfl2k5_read_option_diagnostic_manifest: 1, final-test_apf_b67_writers_native: 1, final-test_nfl2k5_scorebug_source_art: 3, final-test_unif_color_control: 2, final-test_apf_book_unlock_retail: 1, final-test_apf_defense_research_native: 1, final-test_apf_endzone_dxt5a: 1, final-test_apf_field_art_patch: 2, final-test_apf_splb_tag_reassignment: 1, final-test_apf_stfs_roster_rehash: 1. Legacy private-font tests are intentionally retired; old layout tests also require a historic named image/research export or an explicit native-test switch. Optional APF flat-image audits skip when those temporary images are absent; current BASE/TU native jobs use their owned retail inputs.

The ledger preserves preliminary failures: missing registry evidence; an unfinished merge/staging refusal for ignored research; an unsupported validation-plan flag corrected to `--list`; expected stale provider pins/one missing closure module before repinning; and a composition test initially passing unsupported whole-plan keys, corrected to the actual settings surface. The APF installer initially launched isolated child interpreters without Capstone; prepending the prepared private environment to PATH passed all 16 cases without source or assertion changes. See `reports/b71_a6/installer_environment.md` and both installer logs. The first APF suite coordinator retains its nonzero aggregate exit from that initial installer failure; the corrected standalone receipt supersedes only that case. The production manifest failure remains unresolved. Repeated stage/commit labels in the early ledger share the final log path; the initial ignored-path refusal is described above.

## Every recorded command, exit code and elapsed time

Commands run offscreen with `PYTHONPATH=<worktree>:<worktree>/tools` through `reports/b71_a6/run.py`, except where the command explicitly overrides the staged runtime environment. Long jobs use `setsid nohup ... &` under a retained parent `wait` so the sandbox process namespace remains alive; logs are polled. The first bare detached probe lost its namespace before execution and produced no result; its relaunched timed run is the recorded 195.518-second success. No process-name kill command was used.

```text
26e4de8b Compose Arrowhead with colour and reconcile beta 71 integration contracts
dd1cb490 Merge APF5 and preserve its integration handoff
bd878014 Merge APF4 and preserve its integration handoff
eaa6e468 Merge ARROWHEAD and preserve its integration handoff
c65d9277 Merge S3 and preserve its integration handoff
2a8035e3 Apply C5 colour registry metadata before integration
```

Commits use explicit path lists in the private Git directory; merge commits use `--include -- <paths>`. The shared worktree Git metadata remains untouched. Portable incremental bundle: `.scratch/astra-b71-a6.bundle`, prerequisite C5 `a142739b`. The post-commit delivery sidecar records final head/tree, bundle size/hash, verification and fresh private fetch/read-back, avoiding a self-referential bundle hash in this report.

The external integrator must regenerate the production manifest on writable Storage, rerun its manifest/XBE closure, and run the prepared disc builder when authorized. Gameplay must then be witnessed in day/afternoon/night Arrowhead and other stadiums, score/clock/timeout and event transitions, and APF BASE/TU situation-mask/fourth-down cases with Edge/Canary SDL configuration.

ASTRA_DONE
